As is tradition, the Friday before the Grand Prix already had the tournament hall bustling with players trying to use their last chance to earn two byes for the main event. The last chance for them, the first chance for! That is, the first chance for us to see what decks were doing well this weekend. So let's take a look at some winning decklists!

#### Raphael Dolscheid, Grand Prix Trial Winner – Grand Prix Prague 2015
